# Avalara Developer — API Endpoint Index

> Per-endpoint markdown documentation URLs for all Avalara APIs. Each URL returns a standalone markdown file with endpoint description, parameters, request/response schemas, and curl examples. For a curated overview, see https://developer.avalara.com/llms.txt

---

## AvaTax
Bundled API reference: https://developer.avalara.com/products/avatax/api.md
Endpoints: 471

### Accounts
- GET /api/v2/accounts — Retrieve all accounts: https://developer.avalara.com/products/avatax/api/methods/Accounts/QueryAccounts.md
- GET /api/v2/accounts/{id} — Retrieve a single account: https://developer.avalara.com/products/avatax/api/methods/Accounts/GetAccount.md
- POST /api/v2/accounts/{id}/activate — Activate an account by accepting terms and conditions: https://developer.avalara.com/products/avatax/api/methods/Accounts/ActivateAccount.md
- GET /api/v2/accounts/{id}/audit — Retrieve audit history for an account.: https://developer.avalara.com/products/avatax/api/methods/Accounts/AuditAccount.md
- GET /api/v2/accounts/{id}/configuration — Get configuration settings for this account: https://developer.avalara.com/products/avatax/api/methods/Accounts/GetAccountConfiguration.md
- POST /api/v2/accounts/{id}/configuration — Change configuration settings for this account: https://developer.avalara.com/products/avatax/api/methods/Accounts/SetAccountConfiguration.md
- POST /api/v2/accounts/{id}/licensekey — Create license key for this account: https://developer.avalara.com/products/avatax/api/methods/Accounts/CreateLicenseKey.md
- GET /api/v2/accounts/{id}/licensekey/{licensekeyname} — Retrieve license key by license key name: https://developer.avalara.com/products/avatax/api/methods/Accounts/GetLicenseKey.md
- DELETE /api/v2/accounts/{id}/licensekey/{licensekeyname} — Delete license key for this account by license key name: https://developer.avalara.com/products/avatax/api/methods/Accounts/DeleteLicenseKey.md
- GET /api/v2/accounts/{id}/licensekeys — Retrieve all license keys for this account: https://developer.avalara.com/products/avatax/api/methods/Accounts/GetLicenseKeys.md
- POST /api/v2/accounts/{id}/resetlicensekey — Reset this account's license key: https://developer.avalara.com/products/avatax/api/methods/Accounts/AccountResetLicenseKey.md
- GET /api/v2/accounts/mrs — Retrieve a list of MRS Accounts: https://developer.avalara.com/products/avatax/api/methods/Accounts/ListMrsAccounts.md

- POST /v3/invoices/goods/{key}/items-personal-consumption — Send NFe Event Type 211120 
(en) Function: Enables the purchaser to indicate when an acquisition is intended for the personal consumption of a natural person, a situation in which no credit appropriation is allowed. This event must be recorded after the invoice for goods destined for personal use and consumption has been issued. A single acquisition NFe may receive multiple events of this type, each with a different nSeqEvento (cumulative events).
(pt) Função: Permitir ao adquirente informar quando uma aquisição for destinada para o consumo de pessoa física, hipótese em que não haverá direito à apropriação de crédito. Evento a ser registrado após a emissão da nota de bens destinados para uso e consumo pessoal. Uma mesma NFe de aquisição pode receber vários Eventos desse tipo, com nSeqEvento diferentes (eventos cumulativos).

- GET /api/v2/accounts/{accountId}/users/{id} — Retrieve a single user: https://developer.avalara.com/products/vat-calculation/api/methods/Users/GetUser.md
- PUT /api/v2/accounts/{accountId}/users/{id} — Update a single user: https://developer.avalara.com/products/vat-calculation/api/methods/Users/UpdateUser.md
- DELETE /api/v2/accounts/{accountId}/users/{id} — Delete a single user: https://developer.avalara.com/products/vat-calculation/api/methods/Users/DeleteUser.md
- GET /api/v2/accounts/{accountId}/users/{id}/entitlements — Retrieve all entitlements for a single user: https://developer.avalara.com/products/vat-calculation/api/methods/Users/GetUserEntitlements.md
- PUT /api/v2/passwords — Change Password: https://developer.avalara.com/products/vat-calculation/api/methods/Users/ChangePassword.md
